Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in apoptosis theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 194 and the invariant I(A,B) = 47 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 194 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the apoptosis structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 47. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
